The header is\n`records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes`.\n\n`skipped` counts skipped lines in the whole file and is unaffected by `--target`, `--since`, or\n`--until`. Every other column is computed over the records that pass the filters. `first` and `last`\nare the earliest and latest start dates, empty when no record matches. `minutes` and `cost_usd` use\nthe shared formatters from FEATURE-ReportFormat.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-header ===\nIntent: The summary report exits zero and writes the declared header row as its first CSV row.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nHEADER = [\"records\", \"skipped\", \"succeeded\", \"failed\", \"first\", \"last\", \"input_tokens\", \"cached_tokens\", \"output_tokens\", \"cost_usd\", \"minutes\"]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == HEADER, rows[0]\n=== END AC summary-header ===\n\n=== AC summary-single-data-row ===\nIntent: The summary report writes exactly one data row beneath its header, with the same number of fields.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\ndef summary_row(*options):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", *options, \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    return dict(zip(rows[0], rows[1]))\n\nunfiltered = summary_row()\nfiltered = summary_row(\"--target\", \"NoSuchTarget\")\nassert filtered[\"skipped\"] == unfiltered[\"skipped\"], (filtered, unfiltered)\n=== END AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\n\n=== AC summary-records-sum ===\nIntent: For unfiltered and target-filtered runs, records equals succeeded plus failed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nLOG = \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\"\nday = \"2026-10-15\"\n\ndef rows_of(report, *options):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(report, *options, LOG)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    return list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n\nsummary_rows = rows_of(\"summary\", \"--since\", day, \"--until\", day)\nsummary = dict(zip(summary_rows[0], summary_rows[1]))\ndaily_rows = rows_of(\"daily\", \"--since\", day, \"--until\", day)\ntotal = daily_rows[-1]\nassert total[0] == \"TOTAL\", daily_rows\nassert total[1] == summary[\"records\"], (total, summary)\nif int(summary[\"records\"]) > 0:\n    assert summary[\"first\"] == day and summary[\"last\"] == day, summary\n=== END AC summary-dates-match-daily ===\n\n=== AC summary-number-formats ===\nIntent: The minutes column is tenths of a minute and the cost column has exactly four decimal places.\nRequires: executable=go; no row, count, or total is hardcoded in application code.\n- Standard error is diagnostic only and is never compared.\n- The log is opened read-only and is never written.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportCommands.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Commands\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produce the per-command `commands` report with one row per command and a TOTAL row. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-FilterDate.md, FEATURE-ReportFormat.md, FEATURE-LogNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat commands |\n| Consumes    | FormatMinutes, FormatCost, WriteCSV, filtered records from FEATURE-FilterDate.md |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat commands <log>` writes CSV with the header\n`command,calls,failed,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,minutes`. It writes one row per\n`job.command_name` among filtered records, ordered by calls descending and then command ascending by\nbyte-wise comparison. BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportSummary.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Summary\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Produce the one-row `summary` report over the filtered normalized records, with skipped counted over the whole file. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-FilterDate.md, FEATURE-ReportFormat.md, FEATURE-LogNumeric.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat summary |\n| Consumes    | FormatMinutes, FormatCost, WriteCSV, filtered records from FEATURE-FilterDate.md |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat summary <log>` writes one CSV header row and one data row to standard output. The header is\n`records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes`.\n\n`skipped` counts skipped lines in the whole file and is unaffected by `--target`, `--since`, or\n`--until`. Every other column is computed over the records that pass the filters. `first` and `last`\nare the earliest and latest start dates, empty when no record matches. `minutes` and `cost_usd` use\nthe shared formatters from FEATURE-ReportFormat.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-header ===\nIntent: The summary report exits zero and writes the declared header row as its first CSV row.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nHEADER = [\"records\", \"skipped\", \"succeeded\", \"failed\", \"first\", \"last\", \"input_tokens\", \"cached_tokens\", \"output_tokens\", \"cost_usd\", \"minutes\"]\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == HEADER, rows[0]\n=== END AC summary-header ===\n\n=== AC summary-single-data-row ===\nIntent: The summary report writes exactly one data row beneath its header, with the same number of fields.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\")\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0, (code, out, err)\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) == 2, rows\nassert len(rows[1]) == len(rows[0]), rows\n=== END AC summary-single-data-row ===\n\n=== AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\nIntent: The skipped count is identical with and without a filter that matches nothing, because it counts the whole file.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\ndef summary_row(*options):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", *options, \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\")\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    return dict(zip(rows[0], rows[1]))\n\nunfiltered = summary_row()\nfiltered = summary_row(\"--target\", \"NoSuchTarget\")\nassert filtered[\"skipped\"] == unfiltered[\"skipped\"], (filtered, unfiltered)\n=== END AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\n\n=== AC summary-records-sum ===\nIntent: For unfiltered and target-filtered runs, records equals succeeded plus failed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\nimport csv\nimport io\n\nLOG = \"sources/llm_sample.jsonl\"\nday = \"2026-10-15\"\n\ndef rows_of(report, *options):\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(report, *options, LOG)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    return list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n\nsummary_rows = rows_of(\"summary\", \"--since\", day, \"--until\", day)\nsummary = dict(zip(summary_rows[0], summary_rows[1]))\ndaily_rows = rows_of(\"daily\", \"--since\", day, \"--until\", day)\ntotal = daily_rows[-1]\nassert total[0] == \"TOTAL\", daily_rows\nassert total[1] == summary[\"records\"], (total, summary)\nif int(summary[\"records\"]) > 0:\n    assert summary[\"first\"] == day and summary[\"last\"] == day, summary\n=== END AC summary-dates-match-daily ===\n\n=== AC summary-number-formats ===\nIntent: The minutes column is tenths of a minute and the cost column has exactly four decimal places.\nRequires: executable=go;
